[^2204]: [[भनीषोमीये पसी पुरोगाला आशीपोमीयस्य पपया पर्याशीपोमीर्य पारोडाशमे कादशकपाल निर्वपेविति।किस प्रयालेनानि कर्तव्यानि उतानि पौ कृतानि वस्याप्युप. praatfati me on XII 1. l; speriet er en gietara montage fat me मुख्यमन्त्रेण सिद्धिा स्पात् । पाकमेव तम् पुरोगस्योपकुर्यात् । किं कारणम् । तन्त्रमाणे विधानात् । पाकस्य समाप माये पुरोगाशो विहितः, अमीषोमीपस्य पपया ..कपाल Paratitla i prest on XII. 1. 3.|अग्नीषोमीये पशौ पुरोडाशः, अग्नीषोमीयस्य वपया प्रचर्याग्नीषोमीयं पशुपुरोडाशमेकादशकपालं निर्वपेदिति । किं तस्य पृथक्प्रयाजादीनि कर्तव्यानि उतानि पशौ कृतानि तस्याप्युपकुर्वन्तीति । शबर on XII. 1. 1; अथवा नैव भेदेन पुरोडाशस्य तन्त्रं कर्तव्यम् । किं तर्हि । मुख्यतन्त्रेण सिद्धिः स्यात् । पाशुकमेव तन्त्रं पुरोडाशस्योपकुर्यात् । किं कारणम् । तन्त्रमध्ये विधानात् । पाशुकस्य तन्त्रस्य मध्ये पुरोडाशो विहितः, अग्नीषोमीयस्य वपया ...कपालं निर्वपेदिति । शबर on XII. 1. 3.]]

[[P1334]]different [[kiada|kinds]] or of the same kind, there is no necessity of [[&|a]] separate expiation for [[yonial|venial]] sins on the analogy of [[pasupuro dāśa. 2306|paśupuroḍāśa.]][^2205] Vide H. of Dh. vol. [[IV,|IV.]] pp. 88–91 for twelve [[years|years']] expiation for [[brāhmana-murder.|brāhmaṇa-murder.]]

Vikalpa has already been dealt with above (pp. 1249–1252).

The preceding pages are enough to exhibit the main doctrines and some of the principal [[Mimāssā|Mīmāmsā]] rules of [[interpreta. tion|interpretation]] and the great influence that the [[Mimāmgā|Mīmāmsā]] exercised for over two thousand years on Dharmaśāstra works from the Āpastambadharmasūtra to late medieval works like the [[Smrti tattva,|Smṛtitattva,]] [[Nirgayasindhu|Nirṇayasindhu]] and Vyavahāramayūkha. An exhaustive treatment of the numerous technical [[torms,|terms,]] topics and principles of the [[mimāmgā|mīmāmsā]] would easily cover a thousand pages, would enormously add to the extent of this work and has to be given up here for reasons of space, if for no other reason.

The [[Mımāmsā rulas|Mīmāmsā rules]] have been of considerable help to Dharmaśāstra writers. But it should not be supposed that application of the [[Mimārhsa|Mīmāmsā]] rules is easy or that the rules always enable scholars to arrive at agreed or certain and definite conclusions. Apart from the schism [[betweon|between]] the Prābhākara and [[Bhātta|Bhāṭṭa]] schools already referred to above (on p. 1189) there are many circumstances which militate against the certainty and [[Usefulness|usefulness]] of [[mimaṁsā|mīmāmsā]] conclusions. There are variant readings in the sūtras themselves ([[0. g.|e. g.]] on I. 2. 9 there are three readings according to Tantravārtika p. 123, on [[1 . 14|I. 2. 14]] two readings [[&ob.|acc.]] to Tantrav. pp. 128–129). [[Sabara|Śabara]] omitted the [[sutra|sūtra]] II. 4. 17 (vākyāsamavāyāt) and the Tantravārtika notes (pp. 895–897) that [[Sabara|Śabara]] omits six sūtras after [[IIL. 4. 9.|III. 4. 9.]] [[Sabara|Śabara]] frequently mentions a predecessor called [[Vrttikara|Vṛttikāra]] with great respect but differs from him in some cases (e. g. on [[I 1. 3-5),|I. 1. 3–5),]] does not like his way of dealing with sūtras like II. 1. 32, 33 and VIII. [[1. %.|1. 2.]] [[Sabara|Śabara]] gives alternative explanations of the same sūtra (e. g. of I. 3. 4, VII. 4. 13, [[VIII, 1. 39).|VIII. 1. 39).]] Sometimes he treats two or more sūtras as forming one adhikarana and alternatively treats one of them as forming a separate adhikarana (as in [[1, 3.3,|I. 3. 3,]] II. 2. 23–24). He proposes more than two explanations of the same sūtra viz. IV. 1. 2. He puts forward two or more [[explana tions|explanations]] of the same adhikarana (as in IV. 3. 27–28, VIII. 3. 14–15

[^2205]: [[2205. एवमतुल्यरूपेषु पापेषु समानजातीयेषु विजातीयेषु वा गुरुपापोदेशेन वा वार्षिकादिरूपमायश्चिचे कते स्वल्पपापाना परोडाशयावेन प्रसहादेव कालिदेलाववादम The Tartautoernali 94. . p. 85.|एवमतुल्यरूपेषु पापेषु समानजातीयेषु विजातीयेषु वा गुरुपापोद्देशेन द्वादशवार्षिकादिरूपप्रायश्चित्ते कृते स्वल्पपापानां पशुपुरोडाशन्यायेन प्रसङ्गादेव कार्यसिद्धेर्लाघवात् न पृथक् प्रायश्चित्तानुष्ठानम् । प्राय. वि. p. 85.]]



[[P1335]]of two sūtras out of four), IX. 1. 1, IX. 1. [[2-3,|2–3,]] IX. 1. [[34-35,|34–35,]] IX. 2. [[1-2,|1–2,]] [[LX. %. 21-24,|IX. 2. 21–24,]] IX. 2. [[25-28|25–28]] four explanations, X. 2. [[30-31|30–31]] three explanations, X. [[1.|4.]] 1–2 three explanations, X. 4. 1–2 three explanations. On [['Visayo|'Viṣaye]] [[prāyadarsanāt'|prāyadarśanāt']] II. 3. 16 [[Sabera|Śabara]] himself is at a loss as to what the doubt intended by the sūtrakāra is or what the sūtrakāra wants to establish and relies upon what the [[vittikāra|vṛttikāra]] says about the passage to be discussed and about the doubt that arises. Besides, Śabara and Kumārila differ about the subject matters of certain adhikaraṇas as in [[1. 3. 3-4|I. 3. 3–4]] (where Kumārila gives on pp. [[194-195|194–195]] a different interpretation altogether after first accepting Śabara's view), [[1. 3. 5-7,|I. 3. 5–7,]] I. 3. [[8-9,|8–9,]] I. 3. [[11-14.|11–14.]] [[Kumārlla|Kumārila]] shows scant respect for Jaimini by naming him without the honorific title 'bhagavān' or 'ācārya' on pp. 495, 650, 655, 895, charges Jaimini with composing sūtras not containing much substance (p. 895) and remarks that Jaimini's sūtra is improper or wrong (e. g. vide p. 1241 about IV. 2. 27).

Kumārila is often critical of Śabara's [[bhāsys|bhāṣya]] and remarks dozens of times that the [[bhāsya|bhāṣya]] is improper (ayukta) or deserves to be discarded, is unmeaning or absurd (asambaddha) as on pp. 165, [[30%|302]] ([[upeksitavya ),|upekṣitavya),]] 313 (asambaddha), 314, [[66%, 710,731,|662, 710, 731,]] 863 (asambaddha), [[950,953,|950, 953,]] 1090–91, 1615 ([[bahavo doṣah),|bahavo doṣāḥ),]] 1714, 1980, 2004, 2193, 2204.

Another circumstance that makes one entertain grave doubts about the universal validity and usefulness of the principles evolved by the PMS system must also be adverted to. The greatest students of [[Mimārosā|Mīmāmsā]] arrive at entirely different [[conolusions|conclusions]] on many points. Some striking cases may be referred to here. One of the most glaring of such cases is the interpretation of the short sūtra of [[Vagiṣtha|Vasiṣṭha]][^2206] (15. 5, na strī putram dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vā [[anyatrānujñādbhartuḥ)|anyatrānujñādbhārtuḥ)]] 'a woman should not give or take a son in adoption except with the [[asgent|assent]] of her husband.' This has been interpreted in four different ways by works and authors about a Hindu widow's power of adopting a son. The [[Dattakamimāṁsā|Dattakamīmāmsā]] holds that no widow can adopt a son, because, the husband being already dead, no assent of his can be had at the time of adoption. [[Vāoaspati,|Vācaspati,]]

[^2206]: [[Sa ga FH:19 ... Para faceret minat धनधिकारो गम्यते । ...किंच न्याहतिमिर्डवा अचूरवान्धर्व संनिकटमेव प्रतिग्रहीयाव-इति समानकर्तृकतापोधक-सामत्यपश्रवणात होमकर्तुष प्रतिग्रहसिद्ध श्रीणां होमानधिकारवार परि Aura -aldur rela: 1 qu Htet Pp. 19 and 22-23 (Ādan ).|अत एव वसिष्ठः । न स्त्री पुत्रं ... भर्तुः — इति । अनेन विधवाया भर्तुरनुज्ञानासम्भवादनधिकारो गम्यते । ... किंच व्याहृतिभिर्हुत्वा अदूरबान्धवं संनिकृष्टमेव प्रतिग्रहीयात् — इति समानकर्तृकतायोधक-क्त्वाप्रत्ययश्रवणात् होमकर्तुरेव प्रतिग्रहसिद्धेः स्त्रीणां होमानधिकारत्वात् परिग्रहानधिकारः — इति वाचस्पतिः । दत्तकमीमांसा pp. 19 and 22-23 (Ānan.).]]

[[P1336]]a Maithila writer, was of the same opinion on the further ground that, as Vasiṣṭha lays down that one about to take a son in adoption should, after having performed a [[home|homa]] in the middle of his house with the [[vyāhrtis,|vyāhṛtis,]] take (in adoption) only him who is closely related and who is a kinsman and not remote (in habitation &c.) and, as women cannot perform a homa with Vedic mantras, all women including widows have no power to adopt. But in Bengal, it was held that the husband's assent need not be given at the time of adoption and that it may be given long before the actual adoption. In Madras it was held that the word husband in 'except with the [[essent|assent]] of the husband' is only illustrative and that therefore the [[angent|assent]] of the agnates of the father-in-law (husband's father) or of the husband's [[agoatos|agnates]] would be enough to enable a widow to adopt. The [[Vgavaharama yūkha,|Vyavahāramayūkha,]][^2207] the [[Nirnayasindhu|Nirṇayasindhu]] and the [[Samskara-kaustubha|Samskāra-kaustubha]] hold that the [[absent|assent]] of the husband is required for a woman whose [[husband'is|husband is]] living and that a widow can adopt provided she has not been forbidden to adopt by her husband. For a detailed [[pre sentation|presentation]] of these four views and the case law, vide H. of Dh. [[vol III,|vol. III,]] pp. 668–674. The Hindu Adoption and Maintenance Act [[(No. 78 of 1956 ) bas|(No. 78 of 1956) has]] made far-reaching and radical changes in the Law of Hindu adoptions and section 4 of that Act [[overrulos|overrules]] all texts, rules or interpretations of Hindu Law or customs and usages as part of that law, except in so far as they may have [[baon|been]] expressly saved by that Act.

The Mitākṣarā and the Dāyabhāga, both steeped in [[Mimārsā lors,|Mīmāmsā lore,]] differ on numerous points, some of which are:—(1) The Mit. holds that property or ownership arises by birth, the Dāyabhāga denies it and says it is on the death of the [[previ ous|previous]] owner or by partition; (2) superior right to inherit depends on religious efficacy according to the [[Dayabhāga,|Dāyabhāga,]] while [[nearno88|nearness]] of blood relation is the determining factor according to the [[Mit;|Mit.;]] (3) Members of a joint family hold family property in [[quasi severalty|quasi-severalty]] and can dispose of their shares even before partition according to the Dāyabhāga; the Mit. does not say [[80;|so;]] (4) [[800.|acc.]] to Dāyabhāga even in a joint family a widow succeeds to her husband's share on his death without male issue, while the Mit. does not accept this view.

[^2207]: [[Ha a FIT Teratai parerea at fara regenera ज्ञातीनामाज्ञया भवति ... अतो यस्यामवस्थायां भर्चतज्ञा प्राता सैवाबानूपते न खपूर्चा विधीयते । भतो विधवाया भराज्ञा विनाप्यधिकारस। भ्य. म. p. 1133; उक्तविधवसकस्वीकारः अमिरिष diffe fuafunt prel: I. t. p. 160.|भर्त्रनुज्ञा तु सधवाया एव दृष्टार्थत्वात् । विधवायास्तु तां विनापि पितुस्तदभावे ज्ञातीनामाज्ञया भवति ... अतो यस्यामवस्थायां भर्त्रनुज्ञा प्राप्ता सैवानूद्यते न त्वपूर्वा विधीयते । अतो विधवाया भर्तुराज्ञां विनाप्यधिकारः । व्य. म. p. 113; उक्तविधदत्तकस्वीकारः पुंभिरिव स्त्रीभिरपि सधवाभिर्विधवाभिश्च कार्यः । सं. कौ. p. 160.]]

[[P1337]]One may note the great cleavage of [[opipion|opinion]] (above p. 1230) on such texts as Yāj. I. 81 (whether there is a vidhi or a [[piyama|niyama]] or [[parisankhyā);|parisaṅkhyā);]] in note 2111 (p. 1291) between the V. M. and [[Raghunandang|Raghunandana]] both great [[Mimāṁgakas;|Mīmāmsakas;]] in note 2113 p. 1292 on the interpretation of the word 'mātṛ' by Aparārka and the Dāyabhāga; on pp. 1302–3, notes 2133–34 above on [[Bphaspati's Verso|Bṛhaspati's verse]] on re-united coparceners.

In passing it may be stated that to [[oall|call]] the Act [['|'Hindu]] Adoptions Act' is a travesty and [[misnomer, It|misnomer. It]] should have simply been called the Law of Adoptions in India and should have retained the ancient Hindu rules for Hindus as developed by the courts and should have been of a permissive character for all Indians like the English Statute (of 1926) on the adoption of children. The general Hindu law of adoption as developed in the authoritative [[smrtis|smṛtis]] and commentaries and the case law did not contemplate the adoption of a daughter (except when she was made in ancient times a putrikā) and to allow the adoption of a daughter when [[& Bon|a son]] was living throws to the winds the basic [[priniciple|principle]] of the Hindu Law that a sonless man should adopt [[& male as & son|a male as a son]] for the sake of the presentation of [[piṇdas|piṇḍas]] and water (to the Manes) and for continuance of the family name (sections 7 and 11 of the Act). The Act allows a woman (even if not married) to adopt to herself a son or daughter or both and under it a woman, if married, can adopt a daughter even if she has a son but a married woman cannot adopt a daughter if she has already a daughter or a son's daughter living at the time of adoption (sections 8 and 11). The Act does not recognize [[dvyāmusyāyang|dvyāmuṣyāyaṇa]] adoptions which were recognized by Sanskrit texts and also by judicial decisions. The ministers in the [[Govern ment|Government]] and their followers (most of the latter hardly know what Hindu Law was and has [[beon)|been)]] were carried away by [[enthu siasm|enthusiasm]] for equality of rights for both [[mon|men]] and women. As the Indian Union is now a welfare State and it is professed that laws are to be made keeping in view [[&|a]] socialistic pattern, Govt. should have disallowed adoptions altogether or at least put great curbs on indiscriminate adoptions. Instead, they extended the scope of adoption among Hindus by allowing a man to adopt a daughter even when he [[bad &|had a]] son or sons and by allowing even unmarried women to adopt a son or daughter or both. This is against a [[socialistio|socialistic]] pattern. If adoptions had been disallowed entirely or allowed within [[Darrow|narrow]] limits [[oases|cases]] of escheat to Government for want of heirs would have been many and to that

[[P1338]]extent a socialistic pattern would [[bave|have]] been advanced. There is no evidence to show that women were keen on the right to adopt [[&|a]] son or daughter even when they were unmarried. The first [[prinoiple|principle]] of introducing reforms in the existing law is that only strictly necessary changes should be put forward. Reformers and legislators should take to heart the advice of Mr. Aldous Huxley (in [[' Ends|'Ends]] and Means,' p. 30) that change as such is more or [[loss|less]] distressing to most human beings, that man's [[conser vatism|conservatism]] is a [[faot|fact]] in any historical situation and that social [[refor mors|reformers]] should abstain from making unnecessary changes or changes that are of startling magnitude. Legislators should not forget that when they introduce far-reaching changes to lessen hardships, real or supposed, deemed to be caused by [[exist ing|existing]] social conditions, they very often create new problems [[fa|for]] the immediate future. To take a simple example, under the old Hindu Law all girls were to be married before puberty, though Manu [[(IX. 90 )|(IX. 90)]] provided that a girl should wait for three years thereafter during which, her parents or relatives might select a bridegroom for her, but that if they failed to do so she may herself choose her partner. This was not a bad [[provision People|provision. People]] however went in for child marriages for girls and the problem of spinsters never [[aroge. Thep|arose. Then]] over thirty years ago came the Child Marriage Restraint Act [[(XIX of 1929 )|(XIX of 1929)]] whereby the legally allowed marriageable age for girls was fixed at 14. By subsequent amendments and by the Hindu Marriage Act [[(25 of 1955 )|(25 of 1955)]] the lowest marriageable age for girls is now fixed at 15. This Act of 1929 originally went a long way in raising the minimum age for the marriage of girls. Then, after the second world war, owing to economic stringency, and other factors, the tendency of marrying at a late age [[inoreased|increased]] and now the problem of spinsters among Hindus has emerged and great difficulties about the marriages of grown-up women in middle class families are being [[experienood.|experienced.]]

[[P1339]]## Appendix to Section VII Chap. XXX.

It would be helpful to the students of [[Pārvamimamsa|Pūrvamīmāmsā]] and of [[Dbarms. Sastra,|Dharmaśāstra,]] if some of the important and frequently cited maxims ([[ngāyas )|(nyāyas)]] of the former, are brought together in one place with references from the P. M. S., [[Sabara,|Śabara,]] Kumārila, [[Pārthasarathi,|Pārthasārathi,]] the Mahābhāṣya of Patañjali, [[Saṅkarācārya's bbāsya|Śaṅkarācārya's bhāṣya]] on the Vedānta-sūtras, the [[Bbāmati|Bhāmatī]] on the [[Śhaṅkara. bhāsya|Śāṅkarabhāṣya]] and a few others. [[Kamārila, particolarly,|Kumārila, particularly,]] is very fond of employing Nyāyas in the Tantravārtika e. g. on p. 415 (on Jai. [[II, 1, 8)|II. 1. 8)]] [[be|he]] employs [[Sve|five]] different nyāyas. Many of these [[ayāyas|nyāyas]] have been explained in the several volumes of the History of [[Dharmasastra|Dharmaśāstra]] to which references will be furnished at the appropriate places. Several of the [[syāyas|nyāyas]] here mentioned occur in the [[Leukika-nyāyāõjali|Laukika-nyāyāñjali]] (in three parts) [[pablished|published]] by the [[indafati gable|indefatigable]] scholar, Col. Jacob. In some cases his explanations are not [[accurata|accurate]] or satisfactory, but one must not forget that he wrote about half a century [[Aga.|ago.]]

[[अर्के चेन्मधु विन्देत किमर्थं पर्वतं व्रजेत् - शबर on जै. 1. 2. 4 quotes the latter hall also as दृष्टस्यार्थस्य संसिद्धौ को विद्वान् यत्नमाचरेत्. He explains अर्क as a plant ( Calatropis Gigantea ) ; vide also मन्त्रवा, on same p. 111, विश्वरूप on या. III. 243 (first hall); शङ्कर on वे. सू. III. 4. 3 quotes the first half as a न्याय.|अर्के चेन्मधु विन्देत किमर्थं पर्वतं व्रजेत् — शबर on जै. I. 2. 4 quotes the latter half also as दृष्टस्यार्थस्य संसिद्धौ को विद्वान् यत्नमाचरेत्. He explains अर्क as a plant (Calatropis Gigantea) ; vide also तन्त्रवा. on same p. 111, विश्वरूप on या. III. 243 (first half) ; शङ्कर on वे. सू. III. 4. 3 quotes the first half as a न्याय.]]

[[अर्थकुक्कुटविपाक - means the same thing as अर्धजरतीय ; vide तन्त्रवा० p. 720 on जै. III. 1. 13. It means 'it involves a total contradiction to say that one would cook hall a hen for food and keep the other half for laying oggs.'|अर्धकुक्कुटविपाक — means the same thing as अर्धजरतीय ; vide तन्त्रवा० p. 720 on जै. III. 1. 13. It means 'it involves a total contradiction to say that one would cook half a hen for food and keep the other half for laying eggs.']]

[[अर्धजरतीय - Vide महाभाष्य on वार्तिक 5 on पा. IV. 1. 78 ( अर्ध जरत्याः कामयतेऽर्ध नेति ), शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. 1. 2. 8 (यथाशास्त्रं तर्हि शास्त्रीयोर्थः प्रतिपत्तव्यो न तत्रार्धजरतीयं लभ्यम् ), परा. मा. II. 1. p. 702.|अर्धजरतीय — Vide महाभाष्य on वार्तिक 5 on पा. IV. 1. 78 (अर्धं जरत्याः कामयतेऽर्धं नेति), शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. I. 2. 8 (यथाशास्त्रं तर्हि शास्त्रीयोर्थः प्रतिपत्तव्यो न तत्रार्धजरतीयं लभ्यम्), परा. मा. II. 1. p. 702.]]

[[अर्धवैशस - Resembles अर्धजरतीयन्याय. Vide तन्त्रवा. pp. 170, 174, 180, 261 ; शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. III. 3. 18. वैशस means 'destruction, rending into pieces, conflict'. The कुमारसम्भव IV. 31 employs the word in the literal sense.|अर्धवैशस — Resembles अर्धजरतीयन्याय. Vide तन्त्रवा. pp. 170, 174, 180, 261 ; शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. III. 3. 18. वैशस means 'destruction, rending into pieces, conflict'. The कुमारसम्भव IV. 31 employs the word in the literal sense.]]

[[अर्धमन्तर्वेदि मिनोत्यर्थ बहिर्वेदि - Vide शबर on जै. III. 7. 14 and तन्त्रवा. pp. 1083-84; quoted by व्य. म. pp. 115, 146 and notes (by the present author ) pp. 189-191 and H. of Dh. vol. II. p. 1114 and vol. III, pp. 694-95.|अर्धमन्तर्वेदि मिनोत्यर्धं बहिर्वेदि — Vide शबर on जै. [[अवयवप्रसिद्धेः समुदायप्रसिद्धिर्बलीयसी - vide शबर on जै. I, 4. 11.|अवयवप्रसिद्धेः समुदायप्रसिद्धिर्बलीयसी — vide शबर on जै. [[अवेष्ट्यधिकरणन्याय - जै II.3.3 and XI. 4. 10. Vide H. of Dh. vol. III. p. 38 and above pp. 1332-33 n 2201-2, शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. III. 3. 50.|अवेष्ट्यधिकरणन्याय — जै. II. 3. 3 and XI. 4. 10. Vide H. of Dh. vol. III. p. 38 and above pp. 1332–33 n. 2201–2, शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. III. 3. 50.]]

[[अश्वाभिधानीन्याय - इमामगृभ्णन्रशनामृतस्येत्यश्वाभिधानीमादत्ते - तै. सं. V. 1. 2. 1 and the mantra occurs in तै. सं. IV. 1. 2. 1, explained in मी. न्या. म. p. 80, अर्थसंग्रह p. 5. Vide p. 1308 n 2147.|अश्वाभिधानीन्याय — इमामगृभ्णन्रशनामृतस्येत्यश्वाभिधानीमादत्ते — तै. सं. V. 1. 2. 1 and the mantra occurs in तै. सं. IV. 1. 2. 1, explained in मी. न्या. प्र. p. 80, अर्थसंग्रह p. 5. Vide p. 1308 note 2147.]]

[[अश्वकर्णन्याय - vide दुष्टीका on जै. IV. 4. 1 p. 1270. This is put forward to assert that in राजसूय the conventional meaning has to be taken and not the literal one.|अश्वकर्णन्याय — vide टुपटीका on जै. IV. 4. 1 p. 1270. This is put forward to assert that in राजसूय the conventional meaning has to be taken and not the literal one.]]

[[आकाशमुष्टिहननन्याय - vide तन्त्रवा, on जै. I, 3. 12 p. 236 (यस्तान्तुननुपादाय तुरीमात्र-परिग्रहात् । पटं कर्तुं समीहेत स हन्याद् व्योम मुष्टिभिः), शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. [[आख्यातानामर्थं ब्रुवतां शक्तिः सहकारिणी - शबर on जै. 1. 4. 25, अर्थसंग्रह (p. 16, calls it a न्याय), श्लोकवा. (on चोदनासूत्र verse 47 p. 59), तन्त्रवा. on जै. [[आनन्तर्यमकारणम् - Vide यस्य येनार्थसम्बन्धः below. Vide the sutra आनन्तर्यमचोदना (जै. III. 1. 24) and जै. IV. 3. 11 a part of which is 'अर्थतो ह्यसमर्थानामानन्तर्येणार्थसम्बन्धः'.]]

[[आर्य्यधिकरणन्याय - जै. VI. 4. 22; तै. ब्रा. III. 7. 1. 7-8 has यस्योभयं हविरातमाच्छेदैन्द्रं पञ्चशरावमोदनं निर्वपेत्. Here the word उभयं is अविवक्षित and is no part of the विधि.|आर्य्यधिकरणन्याय — जै. VI. 4. 22; तै. ब्रा. III. 7. 1. 7–8 has यस्योभयं हविरात्तिमार्च्छेदेन्द्रं पञ्चशरावमोदनं निर्वपेत्. Here the word उभयं is अविवक्षित and is no part of the विधि.]]

[[उद्दिश्यमानस्य (or उद्देश्यगतं) विशेषणमविवक्षितम् - Vide above p. 1286 notes 2098-99 on ग्रहं संमार्ष्टि and टुष्टीका on जै. VI. 4. 22 p. 1438, on VII. 1. 2 p.1526, on IX. 1. 1 p. 1636, on X. 3, 39 p. 1882 (उद्दिश्यमानस्य च संख्या न विवक्ष्यते ग्रहस्येव ) and व्य. म. pp. 45-46, 90, 132, 210 and विश्वरूप on या. III. 250 ( न च लक्ष्यमाणस्य विशेषणं विवक्षितमिति न्यायः).|उद्दिश्यमानस्य (or उद्देश्यगतं) विशेषणमविवक्षितम् — Vide above p. 1286 notes 2098–99 on ग्रहं संमार्ष्टि and टुपटीका on जै. III. 250 (न च लक्ष्यमाणस्य विशेषणं विवक्षितमिति न्यायः).]]

[[उद्भिदधिकरण - जै. 1. 4. 1-2. उद्भिद्, चित्रा, अग्निहोत्र are names of yāgas (and not गुणविधि) and are प्रमाण. Vide pp. 1244-45 n 2021-22 above.|उद्भिदधिकरण — जै. I. 4. 1–2. उद्भिद्, चित्रा, अग्निहोत्र are names of yāgas (and not गुणविधि) and are प्रमाण. Vide pp. 1244–45 n. 2021–22 above.]]

[[उपक्रमोपसंहारन्याय - If the उपक्रम is doubtful it is the उपसंहार that settles the same and not vice versa. Vide भामती on वे. सू. III. 3. 17.|उपक्रमोपसंहारन्याय — If the उपक्रम is doubtful it is the उपसंहार that settles the same and not vice versa. Vide भामती on वे. सू. III. 3. 17.]]

[[उपसंहारन्याय - जै. III. 1. 26-27; उपसंहारो नाम सामान्यतः प्राप्तस्य विशेषे सङ्कोचरूपो व्यापारविशेषो विधेः । मी. न्या. प्र. p. 261; vide मिता. on या. I. 256, नि. सि. pp. 37 and 71, व्य. म. p. 111 and the present author's notes to V. M. p. 179.|उपसंहारन्याय — जै. III. 1. 26–27; उपसंहारो नाम सामान्यतः प्राप्तस्य विशेषे सङ्कोचरूपो व्यापारविशेषो विधेः । मी. न्या. प्र. p. 261 ; vide मिता. on या. I. 256, नि. सि. pp. 37 and 71, व्य. म. p. 111 and the present author's notes to V. M. p. 179.]]

[[ऋतुलिङ्गन्याय - This refers to the verse यथर्तुलिङ्गानि नानारूपाणि पर्याये । दृश्यन्ते तानि तान्येव तथा भावा युगादिषु ॥ आदिपर्व 1.39 ( = cr. ed. 1. 37 ), शान्तिपर्व 210. 17 (= cr. ed. 233. 15). This verse is quoted by तन्त्रवा. on जै. I. 3. 7 p. 202, in शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. I. 3. 30. It is also वायुपुराण 9. 65, विष्णुपुराण I. 5. 61, मार्कण्डेय 45. 43-44.|ऋतुलिङ्गन्याय — This refers to the verse यथर्तुलिङ्गानि नानारूपाणि पर्याये । दृश्यन्ते तानि तान्येव तथा भावा युगादिषु ॥ आदिपर्व 1. 39 (= cr. ed. 1. 37), शान्तिपर्व 210. 17 (= cr. ed. 233. 15). This verse is quoted by तन्त्रवा. on जै. I. 3. 7 p. 202, in शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. I. 3. 30. It is also वायुपुराण 9. 65, विष्णुपुराण I. 5. 61, मार्कण्डेय 45. 43–44.]]

[[एकवाक्यतान्याय - जै. II. 1. 46. Vide M. M. Jha's ' Pūrvamīmāmsā in its sources' pp. 192-193 for explanation and examples and H, of Dh. vol. III. p. 443 note 744 and pp. 1297-98 n 2123 above. विश्वरूप on या. III. 248 exemplifies this न्याय. The word एकवाक्यता occurs in वे. सू. III. 4. 24.|एकवाक्यतान्याय — जै. II. 1. 46. Vide M. M. Jha's 'Pūrvamīmāmsā in its sources' pp. 192–193 for explanation and examples and H. of Dh. vol. III. p. 443 note 744 and pp. 1297–98 n. 2123 above. विश्वरूप on या. III. 248 exemplifies this न्याय. The word एकवाक्यता occurs in वे. सू. III. 4. 24.]]

[[एकहायनीन्याय - m. by तन्त्रवा. on II. 1. 12 p. 415. It is the same as अरुणा-न्याय above.|एकहायनीन्याय — m. by तन्त्रवा. on II. 1. 12 p. 415. It is the same as अरुणान्याय above.]]

[[एकार्थास्तु विकल्पेरन् - This is part of जै. XII. 3. 10. मिता. on या. III. 257 states ' एकार्थानामेव विकल्पो व्रीहियवयोरिव न च दण्डतपसोरेकार्थत्वम् '.|एकार्थास्तु विकल्पेरन् — This is part of जै. XII. 3. 10. मिता. on या. III. 257 states 'एकार्थानामेव विकल्पो व्रीहियवयोरिव न च दण्डतपसोरेकार्थत्वम्'.]]

[[ऐन्द्रोग्न्याय - Vide pp. 1309-10 notes 1251-52 on ऐन्द्रा बार्हपत्यमुपतिष्ठते.|ऐन्द्रीन्याय — Vide pp. 1309–10 notes 1251–52 on ऐन्द्र्या गार्हपत्यमुपतिष्ठते.]]

[[औपगविन्याय - If a man's name is औपगवि one at once infers, without being expressly told, that he is the son of one called उपगु. Vide शबर on जै. III. 5. 26 p. 1003 and on II. 3. 3 at p. 580 and तन्त्रवा. p. 580.|औपगविन्याय — If a man's name is औपगवि one at once infers, without being expressly told, that he is the son of one called उपगु. Vide शबर on जै. III. 5. 26 p. 1003 and on II. 3. 3 at p. 580 and तन्त्रवा. p. 580.]]

[[औदुम्बराधिकरण - जै. I. 2. 19-25 concerning तै. सं. II. 1. 1. 6 औदुम्बरो यूपो भवति, ऊर्ध्वा उदुम्बर ऊर्क पशवः, तन्त्रवा. p. 352, मी. न्या. प्र. p. 134 ff.|औदुम्बराधिकरण — जै. [[P1342]][[कम्बलनिर्णेजनन्याय - शबर on जै. II. 2. 25 p. 545 (निर्णेजनं ह्यभयं करोति कम्बलशुद्धिं पादयोश्च निर्मलताम् ).|कम्बलनिर्णेजनन्याय — शबर on जै. II. 2. 25 p. 545 (निर्णेजनं ह्युभयं करोति कम्बलशुद्धिं पादयोश्च निर्मलताम्).]]

[[कर्मभूयस्त्वात्फलभूयस्त्वम् - Vide स्मृतिच. II. p. 264 and परा. मा. I. 1. p. 25 (कर्म-धिक्यात्फलाधिक्यमिति न्यायसमाश्रयणात् ).|कर्मभूयस्त्वात्फलभूयस्त्वम् — Vide स्मृतिच. II. p. 264 and परा. मा. I. 1. p. 25 (कर्माधिक्यात्फलाधिक्यमिति न्यायसमाश्रयणात्).]]

[[कलञ्जन्याय - शबर on जै. VI. 2. 19-20 on 'न कलञ्जं भक्षयितव्यं' says that this is downright prohibition (प्रतिषेध) and not a पर्युदास. Vide मी. न्या. प्र. pp. 248-249 and तिथितत्त्व p. 6.|कलञ्जन्याय — शबर on जै. VI. 2. 19–20 on 'न कलञ्जं भक्षयितव्यं' says that this is downright prohibition (प्रतिषेध) and not a पर्युदास. Vide मी. न्या. प्र. pp. 248–249 and तिथितत्त्व p. 6.]]

[[कांस्यभोजिन्याय - This occurs in the पू. मी. सू. XII. 2. 34 itself (अधिकस्य गुणः साधारणेऽविरोधात्कांस्यभोजिवन्मुख्येऽपि ) ; शबर explains 'शिष्यस्य कांस्यपात्रभोजित्व-नियमः, उपाध्यायस्य न नियमः । यदि तयोरेकस्मिन्पात्रे भोजनमापद्यते, अभुरुध्यस्यापि शिष्यस्य धर्मो नियम्येत मा भूद्धर्मलोप इति'।|कांस्यभोजिन्याय — This occurs in the पू. मी. सू. XII. 2. 34 itself (अधिकस्य गुणः साधारणेऽविरोधात्कांस्यभोजिवन्मुख्येऽपि) ; शबर explains 'शिष्यस्य कांस्यपात्रभोजित्वनियमः, उपाध्यायस्य न नियमः । यदि तयोरेकस्मिन्पात्रे भोजनमापद्यते, अनुरोध्यस्यापि शिष्यस्य धर्मो नियम्येत मा भूद्धर्मलोप इति' ।]]

[[काकदन्तपरीक्षाग्याय - Vide दुष्टीका p. 1388 on जै. VI. 2. 1. Some actions such as counting the hair on the skin of an ass or the teeth of a crow are useless or profitless.|काकदन्तपरीक्षान्याय — Vide टुपटीका p. 1388 on जै. VI. 2. 1. Some actions such as counting the hair on the skin of an ass or the teeth of a crow are useless or profitless.]]

[[काकाक्षिगोलकग्याय - Vide तन्त्रवा. p. 198 on जै I. 3. 7, मेधा. on मनु VIII. 1, व्य. म. p. 534, व्य. म. p. 95 and notes thereon at p. 135.|काकाक्षिगोलकन्याय — Vide तन्त्रवा. p. 198 on जै. I. 3. 7, मेधा. on मनु VIII. 1, व्य. प्र. p. 534, व्य. म. p. 95 and notes thereon at p. 135.]]

[[काण्डानुसमय - शबर on जै. V. 2. 3 pp. 1310-11. Vide H. of Dh. vol. II. pp. 739-740, vol. IV. pp. 441-442 note 987 and पदार्थानुसमय below.|काण्डानुसमय — शबर on जै. V. 2. 3 pp. 1310–11. Vide H. of Dh. vol. II. pp. 739–740, vol. IV. pp. 441–442 note 987 and पदार्थानुसमय below.]]

[[कारणानुविधायिकार्यन्याय - तन्त्रवा. p. 245 on जै. I. 3. 16. The qualities of the cause are found in the effect.|कारणानुविधायिकार्यन्याय — तन्त्रवा. p. 245 on जै. I. 3. 16. The qualities of the cause are found in the effect.]]

[[कुण्डपायिनामयनन्याय - जै. VII. 3. 1-4. Vide आप. श्रौ. 23. 10. 6 ff and H. of Dh. vol. IV. pp. 136-137 n 312 for references and p. 1307 above.|कुण्डपायिनामयनन्याय — जै. VII. 3. 1–4. Vide आप. श्रौ. 23. 10. 6 ff and H. of Dh. vol. IV. pp. 136–137 n. 312 for references and p. 1307 above.]]

[[कुशकाशावलम्बनन्याय - Vide तन्त्रवा. p. 268 on जै. I. 3. 24. (कुश is दर्भ and काश is a grassy plant with white flowers). These are so frail that no one can support himself by means of these; so metaphorically it means "supporting by frail or hopeless arguments." Vide व्यय. म. p. 527.|कुशकाशावलम्बनन्याय — Vide तन्त्रवा. p. 268 on जै. I. 3. 24. (कुश is दर्भ and काश is a grassy plant with white flowers). These are so frail that no one can support himself by means of these ; so metaphorically it means "supporting by frail or hopeless arguments." Vide व्य. प्र. p. 527.]]

[[कृत्वाचिन्तान्याय - Taking up a purely hypothetical point for discussion; occurs frequently in Śabarabhāṣya, e. g. on जै. VI. 8. 43 p. 1522 (कृत्वा चिन्तायां प्रयोजनं वक्तव्यम्), on XI. 3. 16 p. 2175, XII. 2. 11 p. 2242; vide तन्त्रवा. p. 287 (on जै. I. 3. 27) and p 890 on जै. III. 4. 1 (यस्तु भाष्यकारे-णोपन्यासः कृतः स कृत्वाचिन्तान्यायेन ).|कृत्वाचिन्तान्याय — Taking up a purely hypothetical point for discussion ; occurs frequently in Śābarabhāṣya, e. g. on जै. VI. 8. 43 p. 1522 (कृत्वाचिन्तायां प्रयोजनं वक्तव्यम्), on XI. 3. 16 p. 2175, XII. 2. 11 p. 2242 ; vide तन्त्रवा. p. 287 (on जै. I. 3. 27) and p. 890 on जै. III. 4. 1 (यस्तु भाष्यकारेणोपन्यासः कृतः स कृत्वाचिन्तान्यायेन).]]

[[कैमुतिकन्याय - The word is derived from किमुत, used in such passages as in कादम्बरी 'गर्भेश्वरत्व ... शक्तित्वं चेति महतीयं खल्वनर्थपरम्परा, सर्वाविनयानामेकैक-मप्येषामायतनं किमुत समवायः'। Vide व्य. म. p. 241 and present author's note on p. 419 of the notes to व्य. म.|कैमुतिकन्याय — The word is derived from किमुत, used in such passages as in कादम्बरी 'गर्भेश्वरत्वं ... शक्तित्वं चेति महतीयं खल्वनर्थपरम्परा, सर्वाविनयानामेकैकमप्येषामायतनं किमुत समवायः' । Vide व्य. म. p. 241 and present author's note on p. 419 of the notes to व्य. म.]]

[[क्षामेष्टिन्याय - जै. VI. 4. 17-20. If the Purodāśa to be offered in दर्शपूर्णमास is partially burnt, still one should perform the rite with the unburnt part of the cake and a prāyaścitta would be necessary only if the whole puroḍāśa is burnt. Vide मिताक्षरा on या. III. 243.|क्षामेष्टिन्याय — जै. VI. 4. 17–20. If the Puroḍāśa to be offered in दर्शपूर्णमास is partially burnt, still one should perform the rite with the unburnt part of the cake and a prāyaścitta would be necessary only if the whole puroḍāśa is burnt. Vide मिताक्षरा on या. III. 243.]]

[[खलेकपोतवत् - Meaning 'like all pigeons, young and old, simultaneously alighting on a threshing floor.' Vide शबर on जै. XI. 1. 16 p 2111, मी. न्या. प्र. p. 65.|खलेकपोतवत् — Meaning 'like all pigeons, young and old, simultaneously alighting on a threshing floor.' Vide शबर on जै. XI. 1. 16 p. 2111, मी. न्या. प्र. p. 65.]]

[[गार्हपत्यन्याय - Same as ऐन्द्रीन्याय above. Vide शबर on जै. III. 2. 3 and अर्थसंग्रह p. 6.|गार्हपत्यन्याय — Same as ऐन्द्रीन्याय above. Vide शबर on जै. III. 2. 3 and अर्थसंग्रह p. 6.]]

[[P1343]][[गुणकामाधिकरण - जै. II. 2. 25-26 based on इन्द्रियकामस्य जुहुयात् (तै. ब्रा. II. 1. 5. 6), the meaning being दधिकरणत्वेनेन्द्रियं भावयेत्. Vide मी. न्या. प्र. pp. 42-43, and 36-39 and p. 1310 above.|गुणकामाधिकरण — जै. II. 2. 25–26 based on दध्नेन्द्रियकामस्य जुहुयात् (तै. ब्रा. II. 1. 5. 6), the meaning being दधिकरणत्वेनेन्द्रियं भावयेत्. Vide मी. न्या. प्र. pp. 42–43, and 36–39 and p. 1310 above.]]

[[गुणमुख्यव्यतिक्रमन्याय - This is part of जै. III. 3. 9 (गुणमुख्यव्यतिक्रमे तदर्थत्वा-न्मुख्येन वेदसंयोगः). Vide तन्त्रवा. pp. 810 and above p. 1290 n. 2108; शाङ्कर-भाष्य on वे. सू. III. 3. 33 quotes this sūtra.|गुणमुख्यव्यतिक्रमन्याय — This is part of जै. III. 3. 9 (गुणमुख्यव्यतिक्रमे तदर्थत्वान्मुख्येन वेदसंयोगः). Vide तन्त्रवा. pp. 810 and above p. 1290 note 2108 ; शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. III. 3. 33 quotes this sūtra.]]

[[गुणलोपे च मुख्यस्य - This is जै X. 2. 63. The words क्रिया स्यात् (or भवति ) are to be understood.|गुणलोपे च मुख्यस्य — This is जै. X. 2. 63. The words क्रिया स्यात् (or भवति) are to be understood.]]

[[गोबलीवर्दन्याय - In such a sentence as गाव आनीयन्तां बलीवर्दाश्च, the separate mention of balivardas that are really included under 'gāvaḥ' is due to the fact that bulls are more intractable than cows and require special attention and effort. This is often used in Dharmaśāstra works. Vide मिता० on या. III. 312-313, स्मृतिच. (on व्यवहार) pp. 66, 67, 102, 169, 280, 300, कुल्लूक on मनु VIII, 28, व्य. म. p. 2 and H. of Dh. vol. III. p. 526 note 966.|गोबलीवर्दन्याय — In such a sentence as गाव आनीयन्तां बलीवर्दाश्च, the separate mention of balīvardas that are really included under 'gāvaḥ' is due to the fact that bulls are more intractable than cows and require special attention and effort. This is often used in Dharmaśāstra works. Vide मिता० on या. III. 312–313, स्मृतिच. (on व्यवहार) pp. 66, 67, 102, 169, 280, 300, कुल्लूक on मनु VIII. 28, व्य. म. p. 2 and H. of Dh. vol. III. p. 526 note 966.]]

[[गौणमुख्ययोर्मुख्ये कार्यसंप्रत्ययः - Vide शबर on ज्ञ. III. 2. 1. This न्याय is also cited as मुख्यगौणयोः ... संप्रत्ययः शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. IV. 13. 12 cites it. मुख्य and गौण respectively mean 'primary sense' and 'secondary sense.' The महाभाष्य has the परिभाषा 'गौण ... संप्रत्ययः' on वार्तिक 1 on पा. I. 1. 15 and on वार्तिक 4 on पा. VI. 3. 46. Vide H. of Dh. vol. III. p. 725, note 1399.|गौणमुख्ययोर्मुख्ये कार्यसंप्रत्ययः — Vide शबर on जै. III. 2. 1. This न्याय is also cited as मुख्यगौणयोः ... संप्रत्ययः शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. IV. 3. 12 cites it. मुख्य and गौण respectively mean 'primary sense' and 'secondary sense.' The महाभाष्य has the परिभाषा 'गौणे ... संप्रत्ययः' on वार्तिक 1 on पा. I. 1. 15 and on वार्तिक 4 on पा. VI. 3. 46. Vide H. of Dh. vol. III. p. 725, note 1399.]]

[[ग्रहै कत्वन्याय - जै. III. 1. 13-15 based on दशापवित्रेण ग्रहं संमार्ष्टि (तै. सं. III. 2. 2. 3). Vide H. of Dh. vol. II. p. 589 note 1380 and above p. 1285-86 and n. 2098.|ग्रहै कत्वन्याय — जै. III. 1. 13–15 based on दशापवित्रेण ग्रहं संमार्ष्टि (तै. सं. III. 2. 2. 3). Vide H. of Dh. vol. II. p. 589 note 1380 and above pp. 1285–86 and note 2098.]]

[[चतुर्धाकरणन्याय - जै. III. 1. 26-27. Vide मी. न्या. प्र. p. 261, अर्थसंग्रह p. 24. Vide vol. IV. p. 670 n 1517.|चतुर्धाकरणन्याय — जै. III. 1. 26–27. Vide मी. न्या. प्र. p. 261, अर्थसंग्रह p. 24. Vide vol. IV. p. 670 note 1517.]]

[[छत्रिन्याय - Vide शबर on जै. 1.4.23 (यथा छत्रणो गच्छन्तीत्येकेन छत्रिणा सर्वे लक्ष्यन्ते), तन्त्रवा. on I. 4. 13, p. 347, टुपटीका on जै. IV. 4. 1 p. 1270 and VII. 3. 7. p. 1552 and H. of Dh, vol. I, pp. 500-501. THE ON. & III. 3. 34 employs it lo explaining fourant (2014. III. 1).|छत्रिन्याय — Vide शबर on जै. I. 4. 23 (यथा छत्रिणो गच्छन्तीत्येकेन छत्रिणा सर्वे लक्ष्यन्ते), तन्त्रवा. on I. 4. 13, p. 347, टुपटीका on जै. IV. 4. 1 p. 1270 and VII. 3. 7. p. 1552 and H. of Dh. vol. II. pp. 500–501. शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. III. 3. 34 employs it in explaining ऋतं पिबन्तौ (कठोप. III. 1).]]

[[a farsauta EUR -- This appears like a not, but it is only an stare for praising with. The pedic passage is in a V. 4, 3.2 and 3. X. 8. 7 deals with it. At on. . UI 3. 18 relies on it.|जर्तिलयवाग्वा जुहुयात् — This appears like a विधि, but it is only an अर्थवाद for praising पयोहोम. The vedic passage is in तै. सं. V. 4. 3. 2 and जै. X. 8. 7 deals with it. भामती on वे. सू. III. 3. 18 relies on it.]]

[[araftav - IV. 3, 38-39 on . . II. 2. 3. 3 Anter 1. Though the performer of the rite is the father, the fruit accrues to the son that is bora. Vide that. OD 9. II. 56 and III. 220, sty. . p. 18, 4. 4. p. 253-54, r. H. p. 136 (Anan, ed.)|जातेष्टिन्याय — जै. IV. 3. 38–39 on तै. सं. II. 2. 5. 3 वैश्वानरं द्वादशकपालं निर्वपेत् पुत्रे जाते. Though the performer of the rite is the father, the fruit accrues to the son that is born. Vide मिता. on या. II. 56 and III. 220, प्राय. वि. p. 18, व्य. म. pp. 253–54, दत्त. मी. p. 136 (Ānan. ed.)]]

[[yerang . IV. 3. I based on pesugta like will yra #OTT ponto (laa. . III, 3.7.2). Such sentences are not Fres, but merely artures|जुहून्याय — जै. IV. 3. 1 based on passages like यस्य पर्णमयी जुहूर्भवति न स पापं श्लोकं शृणोति (in तै. सं. III. 5. 7. 2). Such sentences are not फलविधिs, but merely अर्थवादs.]]

[[Titograrity or pront a - Vide m. p. 860 ( T orrent tuat at wilforrere), nt. ( Te, verso 13). If only the words ... front ware uttered, m ore boing * utere would be iacluded: but if the whole santance be used he would be excluded from the first part. The morning fraqueatly oltog tbla nyāya, 6. 8. on TT 4 00 47. VI. 1, 2 and wire 1 on 9. I, 1. 47 and on afins ? On T. VI. 2, 1; vide Fairt. od T. III, 257 for thla muxim.|तक्रकौण्डिन्यन्याय or ब्राह्मणकौण्डिन्यन्याय — Vide तन्त्रवा. p. 860 (दधि ब्राह्मणेभ्यो दीयतां तक्रं कौण्डिन्याय), श्लोकवा. (वनवाद, verse 15). If only the words दधि ... दीयतां were uttered, कौण्डिन्य being a ब्राह्मण would be included ; but if the whole sentence be used he would be excluded from the first part. The महाभाष्य frequently cites this nyāya, e. g. on वार्तिक 4 on पा. VI. 1. 2 and वार्तिक 1 on पा. I. 1. 47 and on वार्तिक 2 on पा. VI. 2. 1 ; vide मिता. on या. III. 257 for this maxim.]]

[[P1344]][[तरमकपण्याय-3.|तत्प्रख्यन्याय — जै.]] I. 4. 4 [[(तत्पसायं चाग्यशानम्),|(तत्प्रख्यं चान्यशास्त्रम्),]] which means [[सस्प गुणस्प वर्ष मापर्क अयशा यत्रभवति.|तस्य गुणस्य प्रकर्षे प्रापकं अन्यशास्त्रं यत्र भवति.]] [[I0.सं. 1.3.9.1 weread अग्निहोत्रं जदोति (स्वर्मकाम:).|In तै. सं. I. 5. 9. 1 we read अग्निहोत्रं जुहोति (स्वर्गकामः).]] Here अग्निहोत्र is the name [[(नामधेय )|(नामधेय)]] of a rite [[(अपये हो हामो पस्मिन)|(अग्नये होत्रं होमो यस्मिन्)]] and not a [[गुणविधि,|गुणविधि.]] Vide [[मी.ग्या..|मी. न्या. प्र.]] p. 64, धर्मद्वैतनिर्णय [[p. 3.|p. 3,]] अर्थसंग्रह [[pp.4and 20,|pp. 4 and 20.]]

[[वाण्यपदेशन्याय-जै.|तद्व्यपदेशन्याय — जै.]] I. 4. 5. The example is श्येनेनाभिचरन् यजेत. Here the word श्येन [[(meaning 'hawk"]|(meaning 'hawk')]] is applied to the rite called [[syena,|śyena,]] because it resembles the bird in its [[swilt|swift]] action. [[vide|Vide]] मी. न्या. [[म.|प्र.]] p. 238 [[(तेन व्यपदेशः उपमानम् । तदन्यथानुपपश्येति यावत् )|(तेन व्यपदेशः उपमानम् । तदन्यथानुपपत्त्येति यावत्)]] and p. 1245 above.

[[हापूपण्याय|दण्डापूपन्याय]] or [[दण्डापूपिकनीति-|दण्डापूपिकनीति —]] This is very often employed in [[धर्मशान|धर्मशास्त्र]] works. [[Videविश्वरूप|Vide विश्वरूप]] on या. I. 147 and III. 257, मिता. on या. II. 126, [[स्मतिथ.०॥|स्मृतिच. on]] व्यवहार [[PP. 142,146,242,|pp. 142, 146, 242,]] 249, 283, 296, 301, 315, 326, दायभाग [[X.30,|X. 30,]] [[दायतत्व|दायतत्त्व]] p. 170, व्य. म. p. 131 (and my notes on the same at [[pp.221-222).|pp. 221–222).]] For the derivation of दण्डापूपिक, vide अलङ्कारसर्वस्व on [[अर्धापत्ति|अर्थापत्ति]] p. 196 and [[जयरथ bereon.|जयरथ thereon.]]

[[हविहोमन्पाय-जै.|दविहोमन्याय — जै.]] VIII. 4. 1, [[तन्त्रवा. p 11500 जै. 1.2.7.मी.|तन्त्रवा. p 115 on जै. I. 2. 7, मी.]] [[ग्या. प्र. p.149.|न्या. प्र. p. 149.]] In this compound word [[gta|होम]] is the principal word and the word [[art|दवि]] is उपसर्जन (or अप्रधान). Therefore दविहोम is the name of [[arite.|a rite.]]

[[शहराम्याय-|दशहराश्याय —]] Vide प्रायश्चित्तप्रकरण of भवदेव p. 18, प्राय. विवेक p. 81, [[अदितत्व|शुद्धितत्त्व]] [[pp. 240-241.|pp. 240–241.]] There is a [[on|व्रत on]] the 10th tithi of the [[brigbt ball of Jyestha|bright half of Jyeṣṭha]] called दशहरा, since it removes ten sins. The maxim means that in [[cer taio|certain]] cases, by a [[siogle|single]] performance one may be able to secure several objects.

[[प्रयोजनमुत्सृज्य न शक्यमहष्टं कल्पयितुम् ।|दृष्टे प्रयोजनमुत्सृज्य न शक्यमदृष्टं कल्पयितुम् ।]]
[[घटेफले अहरफलकल्पना अन्याया।|दृष्टे फले अदृष्टफलकल्पना अन्याया ।]]
[[हे सति अररकल्पनाऽन्याय्या।|दृष्टे सति अदृष्टकल्पनाऽन्याय्या ।]]
[[बसंभवस्यरस्पाग्याय्यत्वम्।|दृष्टे संभवत्यदृष्टस्यान्याय्यत्वम् ।]]
[[Vide T OD 5. IX. 3, 3 p. 1745, oo #. X. 2. 23 p. 1835 and on X.2, 34 p. 1838,मी. न्या.प्र.|Vide शबर on जै. IX. 3. 3 p. 1745, on जै. X. 2. 23 p. 1835 and on X. 2. 34 p. 1838, मी. न्या. प्र.]] p. 201, [[एकावशीतत्व P.89, भागती on. III. 3.14|एकादशीतत्त्व p. 89, भामती on वे. सू. III. 3. 14]] and [[H. of Db.|H. of Dh.]] vol. III. [[p. 8370 1628|p. 837 note 1628]] and [[abovep.|above p.]] 1260 note 2051.

[[देहलीदीपन्याय-देहलीdoor-sill. Alamp|देहलीदीपन्याय — देहली door-sill. A lamp]] thereon sheds light inside the room as well as outside. This is the same as प्रासाददीपन्याय below. [[भदीपवर|प्रदीपवत्]] occurs in [[जे.XI.|जै. XI.]] 1. 61; vide [[शबर thereon. ज्य. म.|शबर thereon. व्य. म.]] p. 149 explaining या. II. 139 employs it.

[[इयोः प्रणयन्तिन्याय-जे.|द्वयोः प्रणयन्तिन्याय — जै.]] VII. 3. [[19-25:|19–25;]] Relied upon and explained in Mit. on [[Yaj. I. 135, वायभाग|Yāj. II. 135, दायभाग]] XI. 5. [[16p. 194|16 p. 194]] and [[व्यव.प्र. pp. 500-502 and 5358|व्यव. प्र. pp. 500–502 and 535;]] vide H. of Dh. vol. [[III.|III,]] [[pp. 704-705|pp. 704–705]] note 1347a for explanation.

[[घेताकिशोरन्याय-जै.|धेनुकिशोरन्याय — जै.]] VII. 4. 7 (in which the words यथा धेनुः किशोरेण [[occur):|occur);]] [[शपर|शबर]] explains it clearly. धेनुः ordinarily means 'cow', but किशोर means 'foal'. Therefore in [[कृष्णकिशोरा धेनः, धेनु|कृष्णकिशोरा धेनुः, धेनु]] means 'mare'.

[[म तोपशी करोति न सोमे-जै.x.|न तौ पशौ करोति न सोमे — जै. X.]] 8. [[5and|5 and]] XII. 1. [[7. refers to आज्यभागी: vids|7. तौ refers to आज्यभागौ; vide]] व्यवहारसार p. 231 (part of [[सिंहप्रसाद), द.मी.|नृसिंहप्रसाद), द. मी.]] [[p 182 and abovep 1249.|p 182 and above p 1249.]]

[[नविधौ पः शब्दार्थ:-means|न विधौ परः शब्दार्थः — means]] that it is not permissible to hold that [[inaविधि|in a विधि]] [[4744 8 word|वाक्य a word]] therein has a meaning other than [[tho|the]] direct one. The [[कल्पतर on भामती (on..|कल्पतरु on भामती (on वे. सू.]] I. 1. 1 p. 10) explains [['विधायक शम्दे परोलापः शब्दार्थो न भवति,|'विधायके शब्दे परो लक्ष्यः शब्दार्थो न भवति';]] vide [[पर|शबर]] on IV. 4. 19 (which says [[अनुवादेवलक्षणान्याच्या|अनुवादे तु लक्षणा न्याय्या]] न [[विधी)|विधौ)]] and also on IV. 1. 8, where the ten [[यज्ञायुधः (स्य wooden word|यज्ञायुधs (स्फ्य wooden sword]] etc.) mentioned in [[.. 1,6.8.2-3|तै. सं. I. 6. 8. 2–3]] are held to be an अनुवाद and [[nota|not a]] विधि. [[Videपरा.मा. 1.2.|Vide परा. मा. I. 2.]] p. 298 and [[भक. पा.|मद. पा.]] p. 372, [[१च. मी.|दत्त. मी.]] p. 180.

[[P1345]][[नहाश्वदग्धरपन्याय-videar on . IL.|नष्टाश्वदग्धरथन्याय — Vide शबर on जै. II.]] 1. 1 p. 376, [[तन्त्रवा. on जै. I. 2.7|तन्त्रवा. on जै. I. 2. 7]] and on III. 3. 11 p. 818. This is an old न्याय. [[वाविक 16 on पा. I. 1.5018|वार्तिक 16 on पा. I. 1. 50 is]] [[संमयोगीबा नाम्वदन्थरथवर|संमयोगो वा नष्टाश्वदग्धरथवत्]] and the [[महाभाब्य|महाभाष्य]] explains [['तवाचो माटो ममापि रयो दग्धः, उभौ संमयुज्यावहै इति।।.मेधा.|'तवाश्वो नष्टो ममापि रथो दग्धः, उभौ संप्रयुज्यावहै इति ।'. मेधा.]] on मनु [[v.51|V. 51]] and भामती on I. 1. 4. p. 108 mention it. The idea is इतरेतरोपकारकत्व.

न हि निन्दा [[निन्यं|निन्द्यं]] निन्दितुं प्रयुज्यते, अपि तु विधेयं स्तोतुम् [[—vide|— vide]] H. of Dh. II. p. 581 [[a 1359|note 1359]] and above p. 96 [[o 237|note 237]] for passages from [[Sabara|Śabara]] and Tantravārtika. The मिता. on या. III. 221 paraphrases it.

[[नकस्य|न ह्येकस्य]] शब्दस्यानेकार्थता सत्यां [[गतो न्याव्या-vide|गतौ न्याय्या — Vide]] शबर on जै. VIII. 3. 22 and [[Ix. 4. 18|IX. 4. 18]] and the न्याय [['अन्यायश्चानेकार्थत्वम् '|'अन्यायश्चानेकार्थत्वम्']] above p. 1339.

[[नागृहीतविशेषणान्याय-This|नागृहीतविशेषणान्याय — This]] is often expressed in the form नागृहीतविशेषणा विशेष्ये [[बुद्धितत्पद्यते|बुद्धिरुत्पद्यते]] (as in [[शपर|शबर]] on जै. VII. 2. 13) or as [[नामती विशेषणे विशिष्ट केचन प्रत्येतुमर्हन्ति|न ह्यप्रतीते विशेषणे विशिष्टं केचन प्रत्येतुमर्हन्ति]] (as in [[शवर|शबर]] on जै. I. 3. 33 [[P. 304). vide|p. 304). Vide]] तन्त्रवा. pp. 304, 326, 916, [[एका तस्व p. 15, शुद्धितत्व|एका. तत्त्व p. 15, शुद्धितत्त्व]] p. 313, व्य. म. p. 89 (and the present author's notes thereon [[pp. 117-118).|pp. 117–118).]]

[[नास्ति वचनस्यातिभार:-This न्याय occurs very frequently in शबर and धर्मशाला|नास्ति वचनस्यातिभारः — This न्याय occurs very frequently in शबर and धर्मशास्त्र]] works in different forms, but the meaning is the same viz. 'there is [[noth ing|nothing]] too heavy (i. e. impossible to prescribe) for a sacred text.' Vide [[rare|शबर]] on [[ज.11, 2.27 (किमिव हि वचनं न कुर्यानास्ति...भारः), 00 जै. III. 2.3,x.3.11; on जै VI. 1.44,|जै. II. 2. 27 (किमिव हि वचनं न कुर्यान्नास्ति...भारः), on जै. III. 2. 3, X. 5. 11; on जै VI. 1. 44,]] the wording is [['न हि वचनस्य किंचिदलग्यं नाम|'न हि वचनस्य किंचिदशक्यं नाम'.]] [[शङ्कराचार्य on वे. सू. III. 3. 41 and III, 4.32|शङ्कराचार्य on वे. सू. III. 3. 41 and III. 4. 32]] employs it. [[vide विश्वरूप on या. 1.58 (referred to in H.of Dh. vol. II. P.52521224) मिता. 00 याज्ञः IIL298 (H.of Dh. vol. IV. p.66n 161, परा. मा. II. 1 p. 202 and II. 2 p.64).|Vide विश्वरूप on या. I. 58 (referred to in H. of Dh. vol. II. p. 525 note 1224), मिता. on याज्ञ. III. 298 (H. of Dh. vol. IV. p. 66 note 161, परा. मा. II. 1 p. 202 and II. 2 p. 64).]]

[[निमित्तगतं विशेषणमविवक्षितम्-Similar to आय॑धिकरणन्याय. vide|निमित्तगतं विशेषणमविवक्षितम् — Similar to आर्यधिकरणन्याय. Vide]] विश्वरूप on या. III. 212.

[[निमित्तावृत्तौ नैमित्तिकावृत्तिः-जै.|निमित्तावृत्तौ नैमित्तिकावृत्तिः — जै.]] VI. 2. [[27-28|27–28]] and 29. Such passages as [[मिने जुहोति स्कने|भिन्ने जुहोति स्कन्ने]] जुहोति really lay down that whenever such [[animitta|a nimitta]] as breaking occurs a fresh homa has to be performed. Vide [[ÀY. OD AZ|मेधा. on मनु]] XI. 220 [[(एतबदास्तथा &c.)|(एतद्व्रतास्तथा &c.)]] and मिता. on या. I. 81.

[[निषावस्थपतिश्याय -जै. VI. 1.51-52.|निषादस्थपतिन्याय — जै. VI. 1. 51–52.]] Vide H. of Dh. vol. II. p. 46, vol. IV. [[P. 24 a 58|p. 24 note 58]] and above [[pp. 1295-96 and 1 2121.|pp. 1295–96 and note 2121.]] It is employed by [[परा. मा, I. 1. p. 49, पाय. वि. p. 132, ज्य, म. p. 112.|परा. मा. I. 1. p. 49, प्राय. वि. p. 132, व्य. म. p. 112.]]

[[म्यायसाम्य-नि.सि.p.67|न्यायसाम्य — नि. सि. p. 67]] states that rules about [[sraddha|śrāddha]] on a solar eclipse apply by analogy to [[sraddha|śrāddha]] on a lunar eclipse.

[[प क्षालनन्याय - This is expressed in the half verse 'मक्षालनादि पहस्य दूराद स्पर्शनं वरम् ||पङ्कप्रक्षालनन्याय — This is expressed in the half verse 'प्रक्षालनाद्धि पङ्कस्य दूरादस्पर्शनं वरम्']] introduced by विश्वरूप on या. I. 210 with the words तथा च [[लौकिका:. This hall verse is sनपर्व 2.49, which reads मेयोन सर्शन हणार|लौकिकाः. This half verse is वनपर्व 2. 49, which reads श्रेयो न स्पर्शनं नृणाम्]] and the other half is [[धर्मार्थ यस्य वित्तेहा वरं तस्य निरीहता. शारभाब्य|धर्मार्थं यस्य वित्तेहा वरं तस्य निरीहता ।. शाङ्करभाष्य]] on [[के..|वे. सू.]] III. 2. 22 quotes it.

[[पदार्थमावल्याधिकरण-जै. I. 3.5-7|पदार्थप्राबल्याधिकरण — जै. I. 3. 5–7]] on the place of आचमन in view of the words [[वेदं कृत्वा वेर्दिकुर्वीत and explained by शबर 001.3.7.|वेदं कृत्वा वेदिं कुर्वीत and explained by शबर on I. 3. 7.]]

[[पदार्थाचसमय-जै. V. 2. 1-2 and vide काण्डाइसमय|पदार्थानुसमय — जै. V. 2. 1–2 and vide काण्डानुसमय]] above p. 1342.

[[परमतमप्रतिषिद्धममतं भवति-This is like 'silence shows consenty-vide स.भी.|परमतमप्रतिषिद्धमनुमतं भवति — This is like 'silence shows consent' — Vide वृत्त. मी.]] p. 82 and H. of Dh. vol. III. [[p. 669 2 1264 and शानभाग्य on के.. II.4.12.|p. 669 note 1264 and शाङ्करभाष्य on वे. सू. II. 4. 12.]]

[[P1346]][[porary . III, 6. 1-8; certain passages like Try poradt naar OTO ponia occur in . #. III. 5. 7. 2|पर्णमयीन्याय — जै. III. 6. 1–8; certain passages like यस्य पर्णमयी जुहूर्भवति न स पापं श्लोकं शृणोति occur in तै. सं. III. 5. 7. 2]] without express reference to any topic. They are to be applicable to the modifications. Vide [[ft. 1 . p. 117 and part one. 8. 1. 1. 4 pp. 123-124.|मी. न्या. प्र. p. 117 and भामती on वे. सू. I. 1. 4 pp. 123–124.]]

[[97479-.IV. 1. 11 and greichT pp. 1203-5 on the Vedic text efferent पदमीषोमीयं पशुमालभते.|पशुन्याय — जै. IV. 1. 11 and टुपटीका pp. 1203–5 on the Vedic text यो दीक्षितो यदग्नीषोमीयं पशुमालभते.]] The emphasis is that both [[एकस्व and पुंस्त्व|एकत्व and पुंस्त्व]] are to [[be iosisted on.|be insisted on.]]

[[TorteistaTY - . XII. 1,1-6; vide muit o s of warca p. 20, 9r9. . p. 85 and there are's apu ni on the latter and a 2205,|पशुपुरोडाशन्याय — जै. XII. 1. 1–6; vide प्रायश्चित्तप्रकरण of भवदेव p. 20, प्राय. वि. p. 85 and गोविन्दानन्द's तत्त्वार्थकौमुदी on the latter and note 2205.]]

[[engorer4 - Occurs in Sabara's Bbāsya on Jai IX. 2. 3, XII. 2. 16, n. 1. 2. 31 p. 147. ideigor - Meaas grinding wbat is already kell ground and therefore uppecessary repetition (of arguments).|पिष्टपेषणन्याय — Occurs in Śabara's Bhāṣya on Jai IX. 2. 3, XII. 2. 16, तन्त्रवा. I. 2. 31 p. 147. पिष्टपेषण — Means grinding what is already well ground and therefore unnecessary repetition (of arguments).]]

[[Terrara - Literally means 'bending the back again and again to observo each of many objects lying on the ground. Vide IT on $. II. 1. 32 and argallthereon p. 434. The faat. Op 97. III. 216 employs it.|पृष्ठावलोकनन्याय — Literally means 'bending the back again and again to observe each of many objects lying on the ground.' Vide शबर on जै. II. 1. 32 and तन्त्रवा. thereon p. 434. The मिता. on या. III. 216 employs it.]]

[[Asier Regū #8 sa: - Vide ṣut on III. 4. 12 p. 922 and on . XI. 1. 22 p. 2013, aamOn . II. 1. 1 p. 380, III, 1.12 p. 674, III, 4.12 p. 902, III, 7. 10 p. 1080. It occurs io tbe AET3764 on and 2 on YT. III. 1. 67. Vide above pp. 1235-38 n 2010.|प्रकृतिप्रत्ययौ प्रत्ययार्थं सह ब्रूतः — Vide शबर on जै. III. 4. 12 p. 922 and on जै. XI. 1. 22 p. 2013, तन्त्रवा. on जै. II. 1. 1 p. 380, III. 1. 12 p. 674, III. 4. 12 p. 902, III. 7. 10 p. 1080. It occurs in the महाभाष्य on वार्तिक 2 on पा. III. 1. 67. Vide above pp. 1235–38 note 2010.]]

[[warantura - 5. VI. 3. 13-17, pag. (OD HR P. 460 ). It means geproppe पचारेब्रल्यान्तरं प्रतिनिधाय प्रयोगः कर्तव्य:|प्रतिनिधिन्याय — जै. VI. 3. 13–17, स्मृतिच. (on श्राद्ध p. 460). It means श्रुतद्रव्यापचारे द्रव्यान्तरं प्रतिनिधाय प्रयोगः कर्तव्यः.]]

[[marafira A m anda - Vide the rury 'FATI ' etc. above, fara, on 41. III. 263-64 and 288 for this form of the fun.|प्रतिनिमित्तं नैमित्तिकशास्त्रमावर्तते — Vide the न्याय 'निमित्तावृत्तौ' etc. above, मिता. on या. III. 263–64 and 288 for this form of the न्याय.]]

[[Afaqa maut - t. r97. 4. p. 47. The first part of the interpretation of 3 II. 1. 1 by Sabara is so called and the 2nd part is called statufferius.|प्रतिपदाधिकरण — मी. न्या. प्र. p. 47. The first part of the interpretation of जै. II. 1. 1 by Śabara is so called and the 2nd part is called भावार्थाधिकरण.]]

[[fasari gunigfa: - *** On $. III. 3. 14 p. 844; . A. I. 1. p. 391 illustrates it.|प्रतिप्रधानं गुणावृत्तिः — शबर on जै. III. 3. 14 p. 844; परा. मा. I. 1. p. 391 illustrates it.]]

[[भूयसाम्याय or भूयसा स्थासधर्मत्वम्-based on जै.XII. 2.22 (विप्रतिषिदधर्माणा Fat grut Fort HTA). When there is a composite sacrifice comprising several rites the various details of which present condicta the procedure to be adopted is such as to secure the performance of the details common to the largest number; this is relied upon in parter. (on ne p. 498) and 48. F. p. 202.|भूयसांन्याय or भूयसां स्यात्सधर्मत्वम् — based on जै. XII. 2. 22 (विप्रतिषिद्धधर्माणां समवाये भूयसां स्यात् सधर्मत्वम्). When there is a composite sacrifice comprising several rites the various details of which present conflicts the procedure to be adopted is such as to secure the performance of the details common to the largest number; this is relied upon in स्मृतिच. (on श्राद्ध p. 498) and व्यव. नि. p. 202.]]

[[#74 part-. VI. 3 20. The rule is that if a substance prescribed for a sacrifice cannot be bad, a similar substance may be ured (as que for Fra mentioned by Sabara on Jai. VI. 3. 14 ); bat where a substance is expressly forbidden it cannot be employed as a substitute, even when it is similar to the one prescribed. If madga pulse cannot be bad māna polse cannot be used, as Tai, S. V. 1. 8. 1 declares māsa gralns to be unfit for sacrifice. Vide FART. On 97. II. 126, T XIII. 16 (in both this oyāya is mentioned ), $74. . 482, tua. #. p. 555.|माषमुद्गन्याय — जै. VI. 3 20. The rule is that if a substance prescribed for a sacrifice cannot be had, a similar substance may be used (as पूतीक for सोम mentioned by Śabara on Jai. VI. 3. 14); but where a substance is expressly forbidden it cannot be employed as a substitute, even when it is similar to the one prescribed. If mudga pulse cannot be had māṣa pulse cannot be used, as Tai. S. V. 1. 8. 1 declares māṣa grains to be unfit for sacrifice. Vide मिता. on या. II. 126, दायभाग XIII. 16 (in both this nyāya is mentioned), प्राय. तत्त्व p. 482, व्यव. प्र. p. 555.]]

[[form: Anpare-rame as afirmare (. III. 1, 23 ).|मिथःसम्बन्धन्याय — same as वार्तघ्नीन्याय (जै. III. 1. 23).]]

[[fAllsproutnya . III. 1. 22. and pret thereon and na. 4. p. 89. A gupavākya (clause laying down a subsidiary matter) is not to be taken R8 rabsidiary to another guṇavākya, as both are subsidiary to the main purpose and are of equal status. Thoro are two rito via. Agayadhoya|मिथोऽसम्बन्धन्याय — जै. III. 1. 22. and शबर thereon and मद. पा. p. 89. A guṇavākya (clause laying down a subsidiary matter) is not to be taken as subsidiary to another guṇavākya, as both are subsidiary to the main purpose and are of equal status. There are two rites viz. Agnyādheya]]

[[P1348]][[and Pavamina offerings and it is stated that one of these is subordinate to the other. Both subserve the same purpose, viz. being employed jo Darśapūrṣamāsa and other sacrifices. A Vedic text states that vessels made of Varana and Vaikankata wood are fit for sacrifices, but a vessel of Varana wood is aot to be used for homa while one of Vaika kata cay be used. Both classes of vessels are subsidiary to sacrifices, but the text about not usiog Varana vessel in boma is of a gencral character. Therefore, one of the two clauses is not subordinate to the other, Hence Vaikankata vessels are to be used in yāgas in which homa is requisite, but Varapa vessels are not to be used in such yāgas.|and Pavamāna offerings and it is stated that one of these is subordinate to the other. Both subserve the same purpose, viz. being employed in Darśapūrṇamāsa and other sacrifices. A Vedic text states that vessels made of Varaṇa and Vaikankata wood are fit for sacrifices, but a vessel of Varaṇa wood is not to be used for homa while one of Vaikankata may be used. Both classes of vessels are subsidiary to sacrifices, but the text about not using Varaṇa vessel in homa is of a general character. Therefore, one of the two clauses is not subordinate to the other. Hence Vaikankata vessels are to be used in yāgas in which homa is requisite, but Varaṇa vessels are not to be used in such yāgas.]]

[[orari ara1997 - Vide 3* 00 #. V. 4.11 (979... 999 10: Fina) and on V. 3 12 (97... FETTETRATTATO). The idea is: in the case of an authoritative text that much only is to be accepted as covered by it which is expressed by the words used and that it should not be made applicable to otber cases on tbe ground of similarity or apalogy. Vide A . OD . III. 5. 19, TAPI on 8. 8. IV. 1. 4 and on IV. 3. 4; f e 00 HE X. 127 applies this maxim. Vide H. of Db, vol. II. p. 469 a 1101.|यावद्वचनं वाचनिकम् — Vide शबर on जै. V. 4. 11 (याव...कं न तत्र न्यायः क्रमते) and on V. 3 12 (याव...कं न सदृशमुपसंक्रामति). The idea is: in the case of an authoritative text that much only is to be accepted as covered by it which is expressed by the words used and that it should not be made applicable to other cases on the ground of similarity or analogy. Vide तन्त्रवा. on जै. III. 5. 19, भामती on वे. सू. IV. 1. 4 and on IV. 3. 4; मेधा० on मनु X. 127 applies this maxim. Vide H. of Dh. vol. II. p. 469 note 1101.]]

[[GARATE -- This means that the conventional meaning of a word is to be preferred to the etymological meaning as in the case of the word 'rathakāra' in Jai, VI. 1, 44 ff.; vide qir. Ar. I, 1, p. 300. II. 143 about the meaning of the word स्त्रीधन). Vide H. of Dh. vol II. p. 780 note 1508 and the मी. न्या. प्र. pp. 112–113.]]

[[a r YTT - . III. 1. 23. Tai. S. II. 5, 2. 5 lays down that the Vārtraghai verses are recited on Paurṇamāsi and the Vṛdhanvati verses on Anāvāsya These two are prescribed in relation to sacrifices that require the recitation of two apavākyās. There is only one apuvākyā at the Darsa or Paurgamasi rite; therefore these verses cannot be used in Darsapūrṇamāsa, But two apuvākyās are known to be used in the Ājyabbāgas, which are subsidiary offerings in Darsa-Purnamāsa. There fore, the two apuvākyās 'Vārtraghni' and 'Vidhaavati' are to be con Dected with Ājyabbāgas alone and pot with the principal rite,|वार्तघ्नीन्याय — जै. III. 1. 23. Tai. S. II. 5. 2. 5 lays down that the Vārtraghnī verses are recited on Paurṇamāsī and the Vṛdhanvatī verses on Amāvāsyā. These two are prescribed in relation to sacrifices that require the recitation of two anuvākyās. There is only one anuvākyā at the Darśa or Paurṇamāsī rite; therefore these verses cannot be used in Darśapūrṇमāsa. But two anuvākyās are known to be used in the Ājyabhāgas, which are subsidiary offerings in Darśa-Pūrṇamāsa. Therefore, the two anuvākyās 'Vārtraghnī' and 'Vṛdhanvatī' are to be connected with Ājyabhāgas alone and not with the principal rite.]]

[[समं स्यादतित्वात् - This is the पूर्वपक्षसूत्र in जै.x. 3.53-55, but it is held gene. rally applicable unless there is a special or express provision for unequal distribution. [[P1351]][[Bradrar upang - I. III. 4. 20–24. In 2. T. II, 2.4. 6 there is a passage pot Connected with any specific sacrifice viz. two fronte (one shonld wear bright gold). This is a purugadharma and not a sarvapralarage dharma. Vide FART. on T. II. 135-136, where this is used as an arga. ment against the view that all wealth is for yajia,|सुवर्णधारणन्याय — जै. III. 4. 20–24. In तै. ब्रा. II. 2. 4. 6 there is a passage not connected with any specific sacrifice viz. 'सुवर्णं हिरण्यं भार्यम्' (one should wear bright gold). This is a puruṣadharma and not a sarvaprakaraṇa-dharma. Vide मिता. on या. II. 135–136, where this is used as an argument against the view that all wealth is for yajña.]]

[[TORTOTT - Vide 6. III, 2. 16-19 and Terorary (p. 1346) for the meaning of amaton and fit. In these sūtras it is established that the whole that is not to be recited in both Paurṇamāsa-isti and Darsa-imti, but only those portions of it that refer respectively to the deities of the two iṣtis.|सूक्तवाकन्याय — Vide जै. III. 2. 16–19 and प्रस्तरप्रहरणन्याय (p. 1346) for the meaning of सूक्तवाक and प्रस्तर. In these sūtras it is established that the whole सूक्तवाक is not to be recited in both Paurṇamāsa-iṣṭi and Darśa-iṣṭi, but only those portions of it that refer respectively to the deities of the two iṣṭis.]]
